To restore this 4D essence while ensuring the precise robot control, we introduce Kinema4D, a new action-conditioned 4D generative robotic simulator that disentangles the robot-world interaction into: i) Precise 4D representation of robot controls: we drive a URDF-based 3D robot via kinematics, producing a precise 4D robot control trajectory. ii) Generative 4D modeling of environmental reactions: we project the 4D robot trajectory into a pointmap as a spatiotemporal visual signal, controlling the generative model to synthesize complex environments' reactive dynamics into synchronized RGB/pointmap sequences. To facilitate training, we curated a large-scale dataset called Robo4D-200k, comprising 201,426 robot interaction episodes with high-quality 4D annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method effectively simulates physically-plausible, geometry-consistent, and embodiment-agnostic interactions that faithfully mirror diverse real-world dynamics. For the first time, it shows potential zero-shot transfer capability, providing a high-fidelity foundation for advancing next-generation embodied simulation.

</details>


### [224] [When Should a Robot Think? Resource-Aware Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning for Embodied Robotic Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16673)
*Jun Liu,Pu Zhao,Zhenglun Kong,Xuan Shen,Peiyan Dong,Fan Yang,Lin Cui,Hao Tang,Geng Yuan,Wei Niu,Wenbin Zhang,Xue Lin,Gaowen Liu,Yanzhi Wang,Dong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RARRL是一个基于强化学习的层次化框架，用于资源感知的具身智能体编排，通过自适应决策何时推理、使用何种推理角色以及分配多少计算资源，以平衡推理开销与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身机器人系统越来越多地依赖基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体进行高级推理、规划和决策，但LLM推理会引入显著的计算延迟和资源开销，可能中断动作执行并降低系统可靠性。过度推理会延迟动作，而推理不足则常导致错误决策和任务失败，因此需要解决具身智能体何时推理、何时行动的基本问题。

Method: 提出RARRL（基于强化学习的资源感知推理）——一个用于资源感知具身智能体编排的层次化框架。RARRL不学习低级控制策略，而是学习在智能体决策层运行的高级编排策略。该策略使智能体能够基于当前观察、执行历史和剩余资源，自适应地决定是否调用推理、使用何种推理角色以及分配多少计算预算。

Result: 在包括基于ALFRED基准的经验延迟配置文件在内的广泛实验中，RARRL相比固定或启发式推理策略，持续提高了任务成功率，同时减少了执行延迟并增强了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应推理控制对于构建可靠高效的具身机器人智能体至关重要。RARRL通过资源感知的编排策略有效平衡了推理开销与任务性能，为具身智能体的实时决策提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Embodied robotic systems increasingly rely on large language model (LLM)-based agents to support high-level reasoning, planning, and decision-making during interactions with the environment. However, invoking LLM reasoning introduces substantial computational latency and resource overhead, which can interrupt action execution and reduce system reliability. Excessive reasoning may delay actions, while insufficient reasoning often leads to incorrect decisions and task failures. This raises a fundamental question for embodied agents: when should the agent reason, and when should it act? In this work, we propose RARRL (Resource-Aware Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning), a hierarchical framework for resource-aware orchestration of embodied agents. Rather than learning low-level control policies, RARRL learns a high-level orchestration policy that operates at the agent's decision-making layer. This policy enables the agent to adaptively determine whether to invoke reasoning, which reasoning role to employ, and how much computational budget to allocate based on current observations, execution history, and remaining resources. Extensive experiments, including evaluations with empirical latency profiles derived from the ALFRED benchmark, show that RARRL consistently improves task success rates while reducing execution latency and enhancing robustness compared with fixed or heuristic reasoning strategies. These results demonstrate that adaptive reasoning control is essential for building reliable and efficient embodied robotic agents.

</details>


### [225] [Learning Whole-Body Control for a Salamander Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16683)
*Mengze Tian,Qiyuan Fu,Chuanfang Ning,Javier Jia Jie Pey,Auke Ijspeert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用强化学习为仿蝾螈两栖机器人开发统一的关节级全身控制器，实现从仿真到实物的稳定转移，支持陆地行走和水陆过渡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蝾螈机器人多依赖中央模式生成器（CPG）和基于模型的协调策略，而基于学习的关节级全身控制器在仿真到实物转移方面研究不足，且少有腿式机器人在两栖环境中应用学习控制器

Method: 采用强化学习将本体感知观测和指令速度映射到关节级动作，使协调运动行为自然涌现；部署时采用系统级实-仿匹配和仿-实转移策略

Result: 学习到的控制器在真实世界中实现了平坦和不平地形上的稳定协调行走；在仿真中还展示了行走与游泳之间的过渡能力

Conclusion: 强化学习框架能够为高度关节化的蝾螈机器人开发统一的关节级控制器，成功实现仿真到实物的转移，并为理解不同物理模式间的运动过渡提供了新视角

Abstract: Amphibious legged robots inspired by salamanders are promising in applications in complex amphibious environments. However, despite the significant success of training controllers that achieve diverse locomotion behaviors in conventional quadrupedal robots, most salamander robots relied on central-pattern-generator (CPG)-based and model-based coordination strategies for locomotion control. Learning unified joint-level whole-body control that reliably transfers from simulation to highly articulated physical salamander robots remains relatively underexplored. In addition, few legged robots have tried learning-based controllers in amphibious environments. In this work, we employ Reinforcement Learning to map proprioceptive observations and commanded velocities to joint-level actions, allowing coordinated locomotor behaviors to emerge. To deploy these policies on hardware, we adopt a system-level real-to-sim matching and sim-to-real transfer strategy. The learned controller achieves stable and coordinated walking on both flat and uneven terrains in the real world. Beyond terrestrial locomotion, the framework enables transitions between walking and swimming in simulation, highlighting a phenomenon of interest for understanding locomotion across distinct physical modes.

</details>


### [226] [Beyond Cybathlon: On-demand Quadrupedal Assistance for People with Limited Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16772)
*Carmen Scheidemann,Andrei Cramariuc,Changan Chen,Jia-Ruei Chiu,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种按需四足辅助机器人系统，通过共享自主性方法控制，结合半自主任务执行与人类遥操作，帮助行动受限者完成日常任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轮椅附加机械臂方案在增加有限移动性的同时增大了尺寸并降低了轮椅机动性，需要更灵活的辅助解决方案来帮助行动受限者保持独立性

Method: 采用按需四足辅助机器人系统，通过共享自主性控制方法结合半自主任务执行与人类遥操作；实现拾放任务自动化及基于语义的碰撞感知导航；开发口部操纵杆接口用于精确操作控制

Result: 在Cybathlon 2024辅助机器人竞赛中展示系统，并通过家庭实验验证；系统能协助完成多种任务包括灵巧操作；用户研究表明增加机器人自主性可减轻操作者认知负荷

Conclusion: 提出了一种灵活系统，有望帮助轮椅使用者通过解决移动操作问题而无需外部支持来保持日常生活独立性；在主观指标上达到与先前最先进技术相当的结果，同时允许操作者更多自主性和更大操作灵活性

Abstract: Background: Assistance robots have the potential to increase the independence of people who need daily care due to limited mobility or being wheelchair-bound. Current solutions of attaching robotic arms to motorized wheelchairs offer limited additional mobility at the cost of increased size and reduced wheelchair maneuverability.
  Methods: We present an on-demand quadrupedal assistance robot system controlled via a shared autonomy approach, which combines semi-autonomous task execution with human teleoperation. Due to the mobile nature of the system it can assist the operator whenever needed and perform autonomous tasks independently, without otherwise restricting their mobility. We automate pick-and-place tasks, as well as robot movement through the environment with semantic, collision-aware navigation. For teleoperation, we present a mouth-level joystick interface that enables an operator with reduced mobility to control the robot's end effector for precision manipulation.
  Results: We showcase our system in the \textit{Cybathlon 2024 Assistance Robot Race}, and validate it in an at-home experimental setup, where we measure task completion times and user satisfaction. We find our system capable of assisting in a broad variety of tasks, including those that require dexterous manipulation. The user study confirms the intuition that increased robot autonomy alleviates the operator's mental load.
  Conclusions: We present a flexible system that has the potential to help people in wheelchairs maintain independence in everyday life by enabling them to solve mobile manipulation problems without external support. We achieve results comparable to previous state-of-the-art on subjective metrics while allowing for more autonomy of the operator and greater agility for manipulation.

</details>


### [227] [Development of Low-Cost and Bidirectional Syringe Pumps for Soft Robotics Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16803)
*Krishamsu Subedi Chhetri,Aryan Mayor,Elise Corbin,Logan Walker,John Rieffel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出低成本模块化注射泵系统，用于硅胶体素软体机器人的气动驱动，解决传统驱动方法的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统气动驱动方法（高压电磁阀、医用隔膜泵、微型压缩机、压缩流体）存在效率有限、成本高、复杂或精度不足等问题，限制了硅胶体素软体机器人的应用

Method: 开发基于现成零件和3D打印部件的低成本模块化注射泵系统，具备抽真空和泵入空气的双向驱动能力，硬件模块化且软件可定制

Result: 创建了灵活、可扩展的注射泵系统，研究人员可根据需求定制或同时操作多个具有不同参数的泵，为软体机器人技术的研究和教育应用提供了更易获得的工具

Conclusion: 该注射泵系统克服了传统气动驱动方法的限制，通过低成本、模块化和灵活的设计，促进了软体机器人技术在研究和教育领域的更广泛应用

Abstract: Soft robotics leverages deformable materials to develop robots capable of navigating unstructured and dynamic environments. Silicone Voxel-Based Soft Robots (Silibots) are a type of pneumatically actuated soft robots that rely on the inflation and deflation of their voxels for shape-shifting behaviors. However, traditional pneumatic actuation methods (high pressure solenoids, medical diaphragm pumps, micro compressors, compressed fluid) pose significant challenges due to their limited efficacy, cost, complexity, or lack of precision. This work introduces a low cost and modular syringe pump system, constructed with off the shelf and 3D printed parts, designed to overcome these limitations. The syringe pump system also enhances actuation with the unique ability to pull a vacuum as well pump air into the soft robot. Furthermore, the syringe pump features modular hardware and customizable software, allowing for researchers to tailor the syringe pump to their requirements or operate multiple pumps simultaneously with unique pump parameters. This flexibility makes the syringe pump an accessible and scalable tool that paves the way for broader adoption of soft robotic technologies in research and education.

</details>


### [228] [Real-Time Decoding of Movement Onset and Offset for Brain-Controlled Rehabilitation Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16825)
*Kanishka Mitra,Satyam Kumar,Frigyes Samuel Racz,Deland Liu,Ashish D. Deshpande,José del R. Millán*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究实现了基于脑电图的在线双状态运动想象控制，使上肢外骨骼能够直接通过非侵入性脑电信号启动和中止目标导向的伸手动作，提高了康复训练的神经可塑性针对性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人辅助治疗主要作用于肢体层面，仅间接参与受损神经回路，这是实现真正条件性、神经可塑性靶向康复的关键障碍。需要开发能够直接基于神经信号进行控制的系统。

Method: 采用在线双状态运动想象控制方法，通过非侵入性脑电图直接控制上肢外骨骼的启动和中止。引入不对称边界诊断揭示任务型重定心引起的系统性类别偏差，并提出基于注视点的类别无关重定心方法，在不采样命令类别的情况下跟踪漂移并保持类别几何结构。

Result: 8名参与者使用脑电图启动辅助并在轨迹中途自主停止机器人。两次在线会话中，组平均命中率：启动61.5%，中止64.5%。基于注视点的重定心方法显著提高了无阈值可分离性（AUC增益：启动+56%，p=0.0117；中止+34%，p=0.0251），减少了日内和日间偏差。

Conclusion: 该研究弥合了离线解码与实用的意图驱动启停控制之间的差距，实现了精确计时、条件性辅助，与神经可塑性目标对齐，支持未来临床转化。系统能够可靠地传递启停命令，为神经可塑性靶向康复提供了新方法。

Abstract: Robot-assisted therapy can deliver high-dose, task-specific training after neurologic injury, but most systems act primarily at the limb level-engaging the impaired neural circuits only indirectly-which remains a key barrier to truly contingent, neuroplasticity-targeted rehabilitation. We address this gap by implementing online, dual-state motor imagery control of an upper-limb exoskeleton, enabling goal-directed reaches to be both initiated and terminated directly from non-invasive EEG. Eight participants used EEG to initiate assistance and then volitionally halt the robot mid-trajectory. Across two online sessions, group-mean hit rates were 61.5% for onset and 64.5% for offset, demonstrating reliable start-stop command delivery despite instrumental noise and passive arm motion. Methodologically, we reveal a systematic, class-driven bias induced by common task-based recentering using an asymmetric margin diagnostic, and we introduce a class-agnostic fixation-based recentering method that tracks drift without sampling command classes while preserving class geometry. This substantially improves threshold-free separability (AUC gains: onset +56%, p = 0.0117; offset +34%, p = 0.0251) and reduces bias within and across days. Together, these results help bridge offline decoding and practical, intention-driven start-stop control of a rehabilitation exoskeleton, enabling precisely timed, contingent assistance aligned with neuroplasticity goals while supporting future clinical translation.

</details>


### [229] [BrickSim: A Physics-Based Simulator for Manipulating Interlocking Brick Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16853)
*Haowei Wen,Ruixuan Liu,Weiyi Piao,Siyu Li,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrickSim是首个用于互锁砖块组装的实时物理模拟器，通过基于力的紧凑力学模型和结构化凸二次规划解决咬合连接问题，实现高保真度的组装、拆卸和结构倒塌模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互锁砖块组装为接触密集和长时程机器人操作提供了标准化测试平台，但现有刚体模拟器无法准确捕捉咬合连接的力学特性，需要开发专门的模拟器来支持相关研究。

Method: BrickSim采用混合架构：底层物理引擎处理刚体动力学，同时引入基于力的紧凑力学模型描述咬合连接，通过结构化凸二次规划求解内力分布，实现实时高保真模拟。

Result: 在150个真实世界组装体上，BrickSim实现了100%的静态稳定性预测准确率，平均求解时间5毫秒；在动态跌落测试中，能精确复现结构倒塌现象，准确预测破坏发生位置。

Conclusion: BrickSim作为首个互锁砖块组装专用模拟器，为灵巧、长时程机器人操作研究提供了基础平台，支持与多种机器人和现有管线的无缝集成，具有开源特性。

Abstract: Interlocking brick assemblies provide a standardized yet challenging testbed for contact-rich and long-horizon robotic manipulation, but existing rigid-body simulators do not faithfully capture snap-fit mechanics. We present BrickSim, the first real-time physics-based simulator for interlocking brick assemblies. BrickSim introduces a compact force-based mechanics model for snap-fit connections and solves the resulting internal force distribution using a structured convex quadratic program. Combined with a hybrid architecture that delegates rigid-body dynamics to the underlying physics engine while handling snap-fit mechanics separately, BrickSim enables real-time, high-fidelity simulation of assembly, disassembly, and structural collapse. On 150 real-world assemblies, BrickSim achieves 100% accuracy in static stability prediction with an average solve time of 5 ms. In dynamic drop tests, it also faithfully reproduces real-world structural collapse, precisely mirroring both the occurrence of breakage and the specific breakage locations. Built on Isaac Sim, BrickSim further supports seamless integration with a wide variety of robots and existing pipelines. We demonstrate robotic construction of brick assemblies using BrickSim, highlighting its potential as a foundation for research in dexterous, long-horizon robotic manipulation. BrickSim is open-source, and the code is available at https://github.com/intelligent-control-lab/BrickSim.

</details>


### [230] [MolmoB0T: Large-Scale Simulation Enables Zero-Shot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16861)
*Abhay Deshpande,Maya Guru,Rose Hendrix,Snehal Jauhri,Ainaz Eftekhar,Rohun Tripathi,Max Argus,Jordi Salvador,Haoquan Fang,Matthew Wallingford,Wilbert Pumacay,Yejin Kim,Quinn Pfeifer,Ying-Chun Lee,Piper Wolters,Omar Rayyan,Mingtong Zhang,Jiafei Duan,Karen Farley,Winson Han,Eli Vanderbilt,Dieter Fox,Ali Farhadi,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Dhruv Shah,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文挑战了机器人学习中需要真实世界数据或任务特定微调才能实现sim-to-real迁移的普遍观点，通过大规模多样化仿真数据实现了零样本迁移到真实世界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战机器人学习领域普遍认为仿真数据不足、需要真实世界数据收集或任务特定微调才能实现sim-to-real迁移的假设，探索仅通过大规模多样化仿真数据实现零样本迁移的可能性

Method: 开发了MolmoBot-Engine开源流水线用于跨机器人、任务和环境的程序化数据生成，创建了包含180万专家轨迹的MolmoBot-Data数据集，训练了三种策略：基于Molmo2的多帧视觉语言模型MolmoBot、用于直接比较的MolmoBot-Pi0、以及适合边缘部署的轻量级策略MolmoBot-SPOC

Result: 在Franka FR3桌面操作任务和Rainbow Robotics RB-Y1移动操作器上实现了零样本迁移，无需真实世界微调。在桌面拾放任务中，MolmoBot在4种设置下达到79.2%的成功率，显著优于π0.5的39.2%

Conclusion: 程序化环境生成结合多样化关节资产可以产生鲁棒的操纵策略，能够广泛泛化到真实世界，证明了仅通过大规模多样化仿真数据实现零样本sim-to-real迁移的有效性

Abstract: A prevailing view in robot learning is that simulation alone is not enough; effective sim-to-real transfer is widely believed to require at least some real-world data collection or task-specific fine-tuning to bridge the gap between simulated and physical environments. We challenge that assumption. With sufficiently large-scale and diverse simulated synthetic training data, we show that zero-shot transfer to the real world is not only possible, but effective for both static and mobile manipulation. We introduce MolmoBot-Engine, a fully open-source pipeline for procedural data generation across robots, tasks, and diverse simulated environments in MolmoSpaces. With it, we release MolmoBot-Data, a dataset of 1.8 million expert trajectories for articulated object manipulation and pick-and-place tasks. We train three policy classes: MolmoBot, a Molmo2-based multi-frame vision-language model with a flow-matching action head; MolmoBot-Pi0, which replicates the $π_0$ architecture to enable direct comparison; and MolmoBot-SPOC, a lightweight policy suitable for edge deployment and amenable to RL fine-tuning. We evaluate on two robotic platforms: the Franka FR3 for tabletop manipulation tasks and the Rainbow Robotics RB-Y1 mobile manipulator for door opening, drawer manipulation, cabinet interaction, and mobile pick-and-place. Without any real-world fine-tuning, our policies achieve zero-shot transfer to unseen objects and environments. On tabletop pick-and-place, MolmoBot achieves a success rate of 79.2% in real world evaluations across 4 settings, outperforming $π_{0.5}$ at 39.2%. Our results demonstrate that procedural environment generation combined with diverse articulated assets can produce robust manipulation policies that generalize broadly to the real world. Technical Blog: https://allenai.org/blog/molmobot-robot-manipulation

</details>


### [231] [ManiTwin: Scaling Data-Generation-Ready Digital Object Dataset to 100K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16866)
*Kaixuan Wang,Tianxing Chen,Jiawei Liu,Honghao Su,Shaolong Zhu,Minxuan Wang,Zixuan Li,Yue Chen,Huan-ang Gao,Yusen Qin,Jiawei Wang,Qixuan Zhang,Lan Xu,Jingyi Yu,Yao Mu,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManiTwin是一个从单张图像自动生成仿真就绪的3D数字孪生对象的流水线，并构建了包含10万个高质量标注3D资产的ManiTwin-100K数据集，用于大规模机器人操作数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真学习是扩展机器人操作能力的重要基础，但当前缺乏大规模、多样化的数据生成就绪数字资产，限制了仿真数据的规模和质量。

Method: 开发了ManiTwin自动化高效流水线，能够将单张图像转换为仿真就绪且语义标注的3D资产。该流水线包含物理属性标注、语言描述、功能注释和验证的操作建议。

Result: 构建了ManiTwin-100K数据集，包含10万个高质量标注3D资产，每个资产都配备物理属性、语言描述、功能注释和验证的操作建议。实验证明该流水线提供高效的资产合成和标注工作流。

Conclusion: ManiTwin为可扩展的仿真数据合成和策略学习建立了坚实基础，其数据集为操作数据生成、随机场景合成和视觉问答数据生成提供了高质量、多样化的资产。

Abstract: Learning in simulation provides a useful foundation for scaling robotic manipulation capabilities. However, this paradigm often suffers from a lack of data-generation-ready digital assets, in both scale and diversity. In this work, we present ManiTwin, an automated and efficient pipeline for generating data-generation-ready digital object twins. Our pipeline transforms a single image into simulation-ready and semantically annotated 3D asset, enabling large-scale robotic manipulation data generation. Using this pipeline, we construct ManiTwin-100K, a dataset containing 100K high-quality annotated 3D assets. Each asset is equipped with physical properties, language descriptions, functional annotations, and verified manipulation proposals. Experiments demonstrate that ManiTwin provides an efficient asset synthesis and annotation workflow, and that ManiTwin-100K offers high-quality and diverse assets for manipulation data generation, random scene synthesis, and VQA data generation, establishing a strong foundation for scalable simulation data synthesis and policy learning. Our webpage is available at https://manitwin.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [Neural-Symbolic Logic Query Answering in Non-Euclidean Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15633)
*Lihui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HYQNET：一种在双曲空间中执行一阶逻辑查询推理的神经符号模型，通过双曲表示更好地捕捉逻辑查询的层次结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱上复杂一阶逻辑查询推理需要结合符号方法的可解释性和神经方法的泛化能力。现有神经符号模型未能充分捕捉逻辑查询的层次结构，限制了推理效果。双曲空间能更好地表示层次结构，因此提出在双曲空间中执行逻辑查询推理。

Method: HYQNET将一阶逻辑查询分解为关系投影和模糊集上的逻辑操作，增强可解释性。针对知识图谱不完整问题，采用基于双曲GNN的方法在双曲空间中进行知识图谱补全。模型在双曲空间中嵌入递归查询树，同时保持结构依赖关系，利用双曲表示更有效地捕捉逻辑投影推理的层次特性。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，HYQNET实现了强大的性能，证明了在双曲空间中进行推理的优势。

Conclusion: HYQNET通过将神经符号推理与双曲空间表示相结合，有效地解决了知识图谱上复杂逻辑查询推理问题，既保持了可解释性又提高了性能，特别是在捕捉逻辑查询的层次结构方面优于基于欧几里得空间的方法。

Abstract: Answering complex first-order logic (FOL) queries on knowledge graphs is essential for reasoning. Symbolic methods offer interpretability but struggle with incomplete graphs, while neural approaches generalize better but lack transparency. Neural-symbolic models aim to integrate both strengths but often fail to capture the hierarchical structure of logical queries, limiting their effectiveness. We propose HYQNET, a neural-symbolic model for logic query reasoning that fully leverages hyperbolic space. HYQNET decomposes FOL queries into relation projections and logical operations over fuzzy sets, enhancing interpretability. To address missing links, it employs a hyperbolic GNN-based approach for knowledge graph completion in hyperbolic space, effectively embedding the recursive query tree while preserving structural dependencies. By utilizing hyperbolic representations, HYQNET captures the hierarchical nature of logical projection reasoning more effectively than Euclidean-based approaches. Experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that HYQNET achieves strong performance, highlighting the advantages of reasoning in hyperbolic space.

</details>


### [233] [Machines acquire scientific taste from institutional traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16659)
*Ziqin Gong,Ning Li,Huaikang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过微调语言模型学习期刊发表决策，可以提取出超越前沿模型和人类专家的科学品味判断能力，为科学质量评估提供可扩展机制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学进步的关键能力不是推理而是品味——判断哪些未经验证的想法值得追求的能力。这种能力由编辑和资助者日常行使，但从未被成功阐述、教授或自动化。本研究旨在探索AI是否能够从机构记录中提取这种科学品味判断能力。

Method: 1. 在期刊发表记录上微调语言模型，学习编辑的发表决策模式
2. 使用管理学领域的研究提案作为基准测试集，涵盖四个质量层级
3. 比较11个前沿模型、期刊编辑专家小组与微调模型的性能
4. 评估模型在成对比较和一句话摘要任务上的泛化能力
5. 在经济学领域验证方法的通用性

Result: 1. 11个前沿模型平均准确率仅为31%，略高于随机水平
2. 期刊编辑专家小组多数投票准确率为42%
3. 微调模型超越所有前沿模型和专家小组，最佳单模型达到59%准确率
4. 微调模型表现出校准置信度，在最高置信度预测上达到100%准确率
5. 模型能够将评估信号泛化到未经训练的成对比较和一句话摘要任务
6. 方法具有通用性：在经济学发表记录上训练的模型达到70%准确率

Conclusion: 科学品味并非AI无法触及，而是被储存在机构记录中等待提取。微调语言模型学习期刊发表决策能够恢复前沿模型和人类专业知识都无法获得的评估判断能力。这为在质量难以形式化验证的学科中，对日益增长的科学产出进行筛选提供了可扩展机制。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence matches or exceeds human performance on tasks with verifiable answers, from protein folding to Olympiad mathematics. Yet the capacity that most governs scientific advance is not reasoning but taste: the ability to judge which untested ideas deserve pursuit, exercised daily by editors and funders but never successfully articulated, taught, or automated. Here we show that fine-tuning language models on journal publication decisions recovers evaluative judgment inaccessible to both frontier models and human expertise. Using a held-out benchmark of research pitches in management spanning four quality tiers, we find that eleven frontier models, spanning major proprietary and open architectures, barely exceed chance, averaging 31% accuracy. Panels of journal editors and editorial board members reach 42% by majority vote. Fine-tuned models trained on years of publication records each surpass every frontier model and expert panel, with the best single model achieving 59%. These models exhibit calibrated confidence, reaching 100% accuracy on their highest-confidence predictions, and transfer this evaluative signal to untrained pairwise comparisons and one-sentence summaries. The mechanism generalizes: models trained on economics publication records achieve 70% accuracy. Scientific taste was not missing from AI's reach; it was deposited in the institutional record, waiting to be extracted. These results provide a scalable mechanism to triage the expanding volume of scientific production across disciplines where quality resists formal verification.

</details>


### [234] [Knowledge Graph Extraction from Biomedical Literature for Alkaptonuria Rare Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15711)
*Giang Pham,Rebecca Finetti,Caterina Graziani,Bianca Roncaglia,Asma Bendjeddou,Linda Brodo,Sara Brunetti,Moreno Falaschi,Stefano Forti,Silvia Giulia Galfré,Paolo Milazzo,Corrado Priami,Annalisa Santucci,Ottavia Spiga,Alina Sîrbu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于文本挖掘的知识图谱构建方法，用于分析超罕见代谢疾病——尿黑酸尿症（AKU），通过提取生物医学关系揭示疾病的系统相互作用、共病和潜在治疗靶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尿黑酸尿症（AKU）是一种超罕见常染色体隐性代谢疾病，由HGD基因突变引起，导致尿黑酸在体液和组织中积累。由于疾病罕见，相关临床数据和文献有限，且在现有生物医学知识图谱中代表性不足或完全缺失，需要开发专门方法来整合和分析该疾病的有限知识。

Method: 采用基于PubTator3的文本挖掘方法进行大规模生物医学关系提取，构建了两种不同规模的知识图谱，并使用现有生化知识进行验证，利用这些知识图谱提取可能与AKU相关的基因、疾病和治疗方法。

Result: 构建的知识图谱成功揭示了AKU的系统相互作用、共病关系和潜在治疗靶点，证明了该方法在分析罕见代谢疾病方面的有效性，为理解这种超罕见疾病的复杂机制提供了计算框架。

Conclusion: 基于文本挖掘的知识图谱构建方法能够有效整合和分析罕见疾病的有限知识，揭示疾病的系统相互作用和潜在治疗靶点，为罕见代谢疾病的研究提供了有价值的计算工具。

Abstract: Alkaptonuria (AKU) is an ultra-rare autosomal recessive metabolic disorder caused by mutations in the HGD (Homogentisate 1,2-Dioxygenase) gene, leading to a pathological accumulation of homogentisic acid (HGA) in body fluids and tissues. This leads to systemic manifestations, including premature spondyloarthropathy, renal and prostatic stones, and cardiovascular complications. Being ultra-rare, the amount of data related to the disease is limited, both in terms of clinical data and literature. Knowledge graphs (KGs) can help connect the limited knowledge about the disease (basic mechanisms, manifestations and existing therapies) with other knowledge; however, AKU is frequently underrepresented or entirely absent in existing biomedical KGs. In this work, we apply a text-mining methodology based on PubTator3 for large-scale extraction of biomedical relations. We construct two KGs of different sizes, validate them using existing biochemical knowledge and use them to extract genes, diseases and therapies possibly related to AKU. This computational framework reveals the systemic interactions of the disease, its comorbidities, and potential therapeutic targets, demonstrating the efficacy of our approach in analyzing rare metabolic disorders.

</details>


### [235] [NextMem: Towards Latent Factual Memory for LLM-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15634)
*Zeyu Zhang,Rui Li,Xiaoyan Zhao,Yang Zhang,Wenjie Wang,Xu Chen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NextMem是一个用于LLM智能体的潜在事实记忆框架，通过自回归自编码器高效构建潜在记忆，解决现有文本记忆方法上下文负担重和参数记忆方法灾难性遗忘的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实记忆构建方法存在局限性：文本方法带来沉重的上下文和索引负担，参数方法存在灾难性遗忘和高成本问题，需要更高效的事实记忆框架

Method: 提出NextMem框架，使用自回归自编码器构建潜在记忆；采用两阶段训练过程：自回归重建对齐和渐进潜在替换；引入量化技术减少存储开销

Result: 大量实验表明NextMem在检索、鲁棒性和可扩展性方面表现优异，实现了卓越的性能

Conclusion: NextMem通过潜在事实记忆框架有效解决了现有记忆方法的局限性，为LLM智能体提供了高效的事实记忆解决方案

Abstract: Memory is critical for LLM-based agents to preserve past observations for future decision-making, where factual memory serves as its foundational part. However, existing approaches to constructing factual memory face several limitations. Textual methods impose heavy context and indexing burdens, while parametric methods suffer from catastrophic forgetting and high costs. To address these challenges, we introduce NextMem, a latent factual memory framework that utilizes an autoregressive autoencoder to efficiently construct latent memory while ensuring accurate reconstruction. For better optimization, we propose a two-stage training process, including autoregressive reconstruction alignment and progressive latent substitution. We also incorporate quantization to reduce storage overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NextMem achieves superior performance, and excels in retrieval, robustness, and extensibility properties. We release our code and model checkpoints at https://github.com/nuster1128/NextMem.

</details>


### [236] [AIDABench: AI Data Analytics Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15636)
*Yibo Yang,Fei Lei,Yixuan Sun,Yantao Zeng,Chengguang Lv,Jiancao Hong,Jiaojiao Tian,Tianyu Qiu,Xin Wang,Yanbing Chen,Yanjie Li,Zheng Pan,Xiaochen Zhou,Guanzhou Chen,Haoran Lv,Yuning Xu,Yue Ou,Haodong Liu,Shiqi He,Anya Jia,Yulei Xin,Huan Wu,Liang Liu,Jiaye Ge,Jianxin Dong,Dahua Lin,Wenxiu Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AIDABench：一个包含600+复杂文档分析任务的端到端AI评估基准，涵盖问答、数据可视化和文件生成三个维度，基于真实场景数据，挑战性极高（人类专家需1-2小时/题），当前最佳模型仅达59.43%通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文档理解评估基准多关注孤立能力或简化场景，无法反映实际应用中的端到端任务有效性。随着AI文档处理工具在真实场景中日益普及，迫切需要更严谨的评估标准来应对复杂数据分析任务的挑战。

Method: 构建包含600+多样化文档分析任务的AIDABench基准，涵盖三个核心能力维度：问答、数据可视化、文件生成。任务基于真实场景，涉及电子表格、数据库、财务报告、运营记录等异构数据类型，反映跨行业和岗位的分析需求。评估了11个最先进模型，包括专有模型（如Claude Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 3 Pro Preview）和开源模型（如Qwen3-Max-2026-01-23-Thinking）。

Result: 复杂真实世界数据分析任务对当前AI系统仍是重大挑战，最佳模型仅达到59.43% pass-at-1。提供了各能力维度的失败模式详细分析，并识别了未来研究的关键挑战。基准难度极高，人类专家在AI工具辅助下仍需1-2小时/题。

Conclusion: AIDABench为复杂文档分析任务提供了全面的端到端评估基准，揭示了当前AI系统在真实世界数据分析任务中的局限性。该基准为企业采购、工具选择和模型优化提供了原则性参考，并公开可用以促进该领域研究。

Abstract: As AI-driven document understanding and processing tools become increasingly prevalent in real-world applications, the need for rigorous evaluation standards has grown increasingly urgent. Existing benchmarks and evaluations often focus on isolated capabilities or simplified scenarios, failing to capture the end-to-end task effectiveness required in practical settings. To address this gap, we introduce AIDABench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating AI systems on complex data analytics tasks in an end-to-end manner. AIDABench encompasses 600+ diverse document analysis tasks across three core capability dimensions: question answering, data visualization, and file generation. These tasks are grounded in realistic scenarios involving heterogeneous data types, including spreadsheets, databases, financial reports, and operational records, and reflect analytical demands across diverse industries and job functions. Notably, the tasks in AIDABench are sufficiently challenging that even human experts require 1-2 hours per question when assisted by AI tools, underscoring the benchmark's difficulty and real-world complexity. We evaluate 11 state-of-the-art models on AIDABench, spanning both proprietary (e.g., Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 3 Pro Preview) and open-source (e.g., Qwen3-Max-2026-01-23-Thinking) families. Our results reveal that complex, real-world data analytics tasks remain a significant challenge for current AI systems, with the best-performing model achieving only 59.43% pass-at-1. We provide a detailed analysis of failure modes across each capability dimension and identify key challenges for future research. AIDABench offers a principled reference for enterprise procurement, tool selection, and model optimization, and is publicly available at https://github.com/MichaelYang-lyx/AIDABench.

</details>


### [237] [CraniMem: Cranial Inspired Gated and Bounded Memory for Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15642)
*Pearl Mody,Mihir Panchal,Rishit Kar,Kiran Bhowmick,Ruhina Karani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CraniMem是一个受神经认知启发的门控有界多阶段记忆系统，用于LLM智能体，通过目标条件门控、效用标记、有界情景缓冲区和结构化知识图谱来改善长期工作流中的记忆稳定性、整合能力和抗干扰性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统通常像具有临时读写规则的外部数据库，存在记忆不稳定、整合能力有限、易受干扰内容影响等问题，难以满足长期运行工作流的需求。

Method: CraniMem采用神经认知启发的门控有界多阶段记忆设计：1) 目标条件门控和效用标记；2) 有界情景缓冲区用于近期连续性；3) 结构化长期知识图谱用于持久语义回忆；4) 调度整合循环将高效用轨迹重播到图谱中，同时修剪低效用项。

Result: 在长期基准测试中，CraniMem在干净输入和注入噪声条件下都比Vanilla RAG和Mem0基线更鲁棒，在干扰下表现出更小的性能下降。

Conclusion: CraniMem通过神经认知启发的多阶段记忆架构有效解决了智能体长期工作流中的记忆稳定性、整合和抗干扰问题，为构建更鲁棒的LLM智能体系统提供了新方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in long running workflows, where they must preserve user and task state across many turns. Many existing agent memory systems behave like external databases with ad hoc read/write rules, which can yield unstable retention, limited consolidation, and vulnerability to distractor content. We present CraniMem, a neurocognitively motivated, gated and bounded multi-stage memory design for agentic systems. CraniMem couples goal conditioned gating and utility tagging with a bounded episodic buffer for near term continuity and a structured long-term knowledge graph for durable semantic recall. A scheduled consolidation loop replays high utility traces into the graph while pruning low utility items, keeping memory growth in check and reducing interference. On long horizon benchmarks evaluated under both clean inputs and injected noise, CraniMem is more robust than a Vanilla RAG and Mem0 baseline and exhibits smaller performance drops under distraction. Our code is available at https://github.com/PearlMody05/Cranimem and the accompanying PyPI package at https://pypi.org/project/cranimem.

</details>


### [238] [GSI Agent: Domain Knowledge Enhancement for Large Language Models in Green Stormwater Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15643)
*Shaohuang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GSI Agent框架，通过监督微调、检索增强生成和智能体推理管道三种策略，增强LLM在绿色雨水基础设施领域的专业能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绿色雨水基础设施需要持续检查维护，但领域知识分散在市政手册等文档中，非专家用户难以获取可靠指导。通用LLM缺乏领域专业知识，在工程场景中可能产生不准确回答，限制了其在专业基础设施任务中的应用。

Method: 提出GSI Agent框架，整合三种互补策略：1) 在精心策划的GSI指令数据集上进行监督微调；2) 基于市政文档构建的内部GSI知识库进行检索增强生成；3) 协调检索、上下文集成和结构化响应生成的智能体推理管道。还构建了与真实世界GSI检查维护场景对齐的新数据集。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架显著提升了领域特定性能，同时保持通用知识能力。在GSI数据集上，BLEU-4从0.090提升到0.307；在通用知识数据集上性能保持稳定（0.304 vs. 0.305）。

Conclusion: 系统性的领域知识增强能够有效将通用LLM适配到专业基础设施应用中，为工程领域的专业AI助手开发提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Green Stormwater Infrastructure (GSI) systems, such as permeable pavement, rain gardens, and bioretention facilities, require continuous inspection and maintenance to ensure long-term performance. However, domain knowledge about GSI is often scattered across municipal manuals, regulatory documents, and inspection forms. As a result, non-expert users and maintenance staff may struggle to obtain reliable and actionable guidance from field observations. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong general reasoning and language generation capabilities, they often lack domain-specific knowledge and may produce inaccurate or hallucinated answers in engineering scenarios. This limitation restricts their direct application to professional infrastructure tasks. In this paper, we propose GSI Agent, a domain-enhanced LLM framework designed to improve performance in GSI-related tasks. Our approach integrates three complementary strategies: (1) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a curated GSI instruction dataset, (2) retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) over an internal GSI knowledge base constructed from municipal documents, and (3) an agent-based reasoning pipeline that coordinates retrieval, context integration, and structured response generation. We also construct a new GSI Dataset aligned with real-world GSI inspection and maintenance scenarios. Experimental results show that our framework significantly improves domain-specific performance while maintaining general knowledge capability. On the GSI dataset, BLEU-4 improves from 0.090 to 0.307, while performance on the common knowledge dataset remains stable (0.304 vs. 0.305). These results demonstrate that systematic domain knowledge enhancement can effectively adapt general-purpose LLMs to professional infrastructure applications.

</details>


### [239] [QV May Be Enough: Toward the Essence of Attention in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15665)
*Zhang Edward*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从语言学的词性和句法分析角度，推导出Transformer中QKV机制的本质，提出QV范式及QV-Ka优化方案，为LLM架构演进提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从第一性原理和语言学视角出发，深入理解Transformer架构中QKV机制的本质，为当前各种注意力变体（MQA、GQA、MLA等）提供统一解释框架，并探索优化方向。

Method: 基于词性(POS)和句法分析的语言学理论，推导QKV机制的本质；提出QV范式并通过实验验证；进一步提出QV-Ka优化方案并进行实验验证。

Result: 建立了QKV机制的可解释理论分析框架，为MQA、GQA、MLA等架构提供了统一解释；验证了QV范式的有效性；实验证明了QV-Ka优化方案的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的QKV机制理论分析为大语言模型架构的未来演进奠定了坚实基础，提供了可解释的理论框架和优化方向。

Abstract: Starting from first principles and a linguistic perspective centered on part-of-speech (POS) and syntactic analysis, this paper explores and derives the underlying essence of the Query-Key-Value (QKV) mechanism within the Transformer architecture. Based on this theoretical foundation, we provide a unified explanatory framework for the efficacy of contemporary architectures, including MQA, GQA, and MLA, while identifying their inherent trade-offs and potential optimization trajectories. We introduce the QV paradigm and provide empirical evidence for its validity. Building upon this, we propose the QV-Ka optimization scheme, which is further substantiated through experimental validation. The interpretable theoretical analysis of the QKV mechanism presented in this work establishes a robust foundation for the future evolution of large language model architectures.

</details>


### [240] [Compiled Memory: Not More Information, but More Precise Instructions for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15666)
*James Rhodes,George Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Atlas是一个记忆内核，将任务经验编译到智能体指令结构中，通过指令重写而非上下文注入来提升性能，在多个任务上显著改进模型表现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言智能体记忆系统主要关注内存管理（如何检索和分页信息），但忽略了内存效用问题——什么经验值得保留以及如何改变智能体行为

Method: 提出Atlas记忆内核，通过三步验证门从智能体失败和成功中提取事实，通过重写系统提示来传递学习到的子要点，无需微调、RAG或人工干预

Result: 在CUAD合同分析任务上，GPT-4o的token级F1提升+8.7pp，精确率提升+12.5pp；在HotpotQA多跳问答任务上，联合F1提升+3.16pp；相同编译提示应用于Claude Sonnet 4.5也获得+2.31pp提升

Conclusion: 记忆是蒸馏而非存储，传递是指令重写而非上下文注入；编译的知识是任务形态而非模型形态，具有跨模型泛化能力

Abstract: Existing memory systems for language agents address memory management: how to retrieve and page more information within a context budget. We address a complementary problem -- memory utility: what experience is worth keeping, and how it should change agent behavior. We present Atlas, a memory kernel that compiles accumulated task experience into an agent's instruction structure -- without fine-tuning, RAG, or human intervention. Memory is distillation, not storage; delivery is instruction rewriting, not context injection. Facts extracted from agent failures and successes are verified through a three-step promotion gate and delivered by rewriting the agent's system prompt with learned sub-bullets. On CUAD contract analysis, the evolved prompt improves GPT-4o token-level F1 by $+8.7$pp and precision by $+12.5$pp. On HotpotQA multi-hop QA, joint F1 improves $+3.16$pp. An ablation isolates the mechanism's defining property -- the training signal constraint: the evolved prompt learns exactly what it is taught, and nothing more. Applied to Claude Sonnet~4.5 using the same evolved prompt -- compiled from GPT-4o errors, unchanged -- joint F1 improves $+2.31$pp, with gains concentrating where Claude's stronger baseline leaves the most room -- confirming that the compiled knowledge is task-shaped, not model-shaped.

</details>


### [241] [A Dynamic Survey of Fuzzy, Intuitionistic Fuzzy, Neutrosophic, Plithogenic, and Extensional Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15667)
*Takaaki Fujita,Florentin Smarandache*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于模糊集、直觉模糊集、中智集和Plithogenic集等不确定性建模框架的综合性综述书籍，旨在系统梳理现有发展并提供统一阐述以促进新见解和应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界现象常表现出模糊性、部分真实性和不完全信息，需要数学上严谨的广义集合论框架来建模这种不确定性。虽然已有多种框架被提出，但文献分散且概念重复，需要一个系统性的综述来统一阐述这四个主要的不确定性建模家族。

Method: 采用大规模综合性调查方法，系统梳理模糊集、直觉模糊集、中智集和Plithogenic集这四个主要不确定性建模框架的文献。通过统一阐述的方式，比较分析这些框架中的概念、构造和结构模式。

Result: 提供了对这四个不确定性建模框架的全面综述，揭示了它们之间的概念重复和结构模式相似性。通过统一阐述，为读者提供了系统化的知识框架，有助于理解这些理论的发展脉络和相互关系。

Conclusion: 本书通过系统梳理模糊集、直觉模糊集、中智集和Plithogenic集这四个主要不确定性建模框架，为读者提供了全面的知识概览。这种统一阐述不仅有助于理解现有发展，还能激发新的见解、概念扩展和跨学科应用，推动不确定性建模理论的进一步发展。

Abstract: Real-world phenomena often exhibit vagueness, partial truth, and incomplete information. To model such uncertainty in a mathematically rigorous way, many generalized set-theoretic frameworks have been introduced, including Fuzzy Sets [1], Intuitionistic Fuzzy Sets [2], Neutrosophic Sets [3,4], Vague Sets [5], Hesitant Fuzzy Sets [6], Picture Fuzzy Sets [7], Quadripartitioned Neutrosophic Sets [8], Penta-Partitioned Neutrosophic Sets [9], Plithogenic Sets [10], HyperFuzzy Sets [11], and HyperNeutrosophic Sets [12]. Within these frameworks, a wide range of notions has been proposed and studied, particularly in the settings of fuzzy, intuitionistic fuzzy, neutrosophic, and plithogenic set theories. This extensive literature underscores both the significance of these theories and the breadth of their application areas. As a result, many ideas, constructions, and structural patterns recur across these four major families of uncertainty-oriented models. In this book, we provide a comprehensive, large-scale survey of Fuzzy, Intuitionistic Fuzzy, Neutrosophic, and Plithogenic Sets. Our goal is to give readers a systematic overview of existing developments and, through a unified exposition, to stimulate new insights, further conceptual extensions, and additional applications across a wide range of disciplines.

</details>


### [242] [Quantum-Secure-By-Construction (QSC): A Paradigm Shift For Post-Quantum Agentic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15668)
*Arit Kumar Bishwas,Mousumi Sen,Albert Nieto-Morales,Joel Jacob Varghese*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QSC（量子安全构造）是一种设计范式，将量子安全通信作为智能体AI系统的核心架构属性，而非后期升级。它通过运行时自适应安全模型，结合后量子密码学、量子随机数生成和量子密钥分发，为跨异构环境的自主智能体交互提供安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体AI系统在全球分布式和长寿命基础设施中扩展，安全且符合策略的通信成为基本系统挑战。在量子时代，当前AI部署中内置的密码学假设可能在其运行寿命期内失效，这一问题变得更加严重。

Method: QSC通过运行时自适应安全模型实现，该模型结合后量子密码学、量子随机数生成和量子密钥分发。采用密码学可插拔架构，由策略引导，允许系统根据基础设施可用性、监管约束和性能需求调整安全态势。包含治理感知的编排层，在智能体全生命周期中选择和组合特定链路的密码学保护。

Result: 系统级分析和实证评估表明，QSC能够减少将量子安全引入已部署智能体AI系统的操作复杂性和成本。它展示了经典与量子安全机制之间的权衡，为后量子智能体智能奠定了基础。

Conclusion: QSC作为后量子智能体智能的基础范式，为设计全球可互操作、有弹性且面向未来的智能系统建立了原则性路径。它将量子安全通信从后期升级转变为核心架构属性，解决了量子时代智能体AI系统的安全挑战。

Abstract: As agentic artificial intelligence systems scale across globally distributed and long lived infrastructures, secure and policy compliant communication becomes a fundamental systems challenge. This challenge grows more serious in the quantum era, where the cryptographic assumptions built into today's AI deployments may not remain valid over their operational lifetime. Here, we introduce quantum secure by construction, or QSC, as a design paradigm that treats quantum secure communication as a core architectural property of agentic AI systems rather than an upgrade added later. We realize QSC through a runtime adaptive security model that combines post quantum cryptography, quantum random number generation, and quantum key distribution to secure interactions among autonomous agents operating across heterogeneous cloud, edge, and inter organizational environments. The approach is cryptographically pluggable and guided by policy, allowing the system to adjust its security posture according to infrastructure availability, regulatory constraints, and performance needs. QSC contributes a governance aware orchestration layer that selects and combines link specific cryptographic protections across the full agent lifecycle, including session bootstrap, inter agent coordination, tool invocation, and memory access. Through system level analysis and empirical evaluation, we examine the trade offs between classical and quantum secure mechanisms and show that QSC can reduce the operational complexity and cost of introducing quantum security into deployed agentic AI systems. These results position QSC as a foundational paradigm for post quantum agentic intelligence and establish a principled pathway for designing globally interoperable, resilient, and future ready intelligent systems.

</details>


### [243] [Theoretical Foundations of Latent Posterior Factors: Formal Guarantees for Multi-Evidence Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15674)
*Aliyu Agboola Alege*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LPF是一个用于多证据概率预测的理论框架，通过变分自编码器编码证据为高斯后验，转换为软因子，并通过精确推理或学习聚合器进行聚合，具有七个形式化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多证据推理在医疗诊断、金融风险评估、法律案件分析等高风险领域普遍存在，但现有方法要么缺乏形式化保证，要么无法在架构上处理多证据场景。

Method: LPF通过变分自编码器将每个证据项编码为高斯潜在后验，通过蒙特卡洛边缘化将后验转换为软因子，然后通过精确的Sum-Product Network推理（LPF-SPN）或学习的神经聚合器（LPF-Learned）聚合因子。

Result: 证明了七个形式化保证：校准保持（ECE ≤ ε + C/√K_eff）、蒙特卡洛误差以O(1/√M)衰减、非空PAC-Bayes边界（N=4200时训练-测试差距为0.0085）、在信息论下界1.12倍内运行、在腐败下以O(εδ√K)优雅退化（一半证据被对抗替换时保持88%性能）、校准衰减为O(1/√K)（R²=0.849）、以及误差低于0.002%的精确认知-偶然不确定性分解。

Conclusion: LPF的理论框架为安全关键应用中的可信多证据AI奠定了基础，所有定理在包含多达4200个训练样本的受控数据集上得到实证验证。

Abstract: We present a complete theoretical characterization of Latent Posterior Factors (LPF), a principled framework for aggregating multiple heterogeneous evidence items in probabilistic prediction tasks. Multi-evidence reasoning arises pervasively in high-stakes domains including healthcare diagnosis, financial risk assessment, legal case analysis, and regulatory compliance, yet existing approaches either lack formal guarantees or fail to handle multi-evidence scenarios architecturally. LPF encodes each evidence item into a Gaussian latent posterior via a variational autoencoder, converting posteriors to soft factors through Monte Carlo marginalization, and aggregating factors via exact Sum-Product Network inference (LPF-SPN) or a learned neural aggregator (LPF-Learned).
  We prove seven formal guarantees spanning the key desiderata for trustworthy AI: Calibration Preservation (ECE <= epsilon + C/sqrt(K_eff)); Monte Carlo Error decaying as O(1/sqrt(M)); a non-vacuous PAC-Bayes bound with train-test gap of 0.0085 at N=4200; operation within 1.12x of the information-theoretic lower bound; graceful degradation as O(epsilon*delta*sqrt(K)) under corruption, maintaining 88% performance with half of evidence adversarially replaced; O(1/sqrt(K)) calibration decay with R^2=0.849; and exact epistemic-aleatoric uncertainty decomposition with error below 0.002%. All theorems are empirically validated on controlled datasets spanning up to 4,200 training examples. Our theoretical framework establishes LPF as a foundation for trustworthy multi-evidence AI in safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [244] [Survey of Various Fuzzy and Uncertain Decision-Making Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15709)
*Takaaki Fujita,Florentin Smarandache*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该调查论文回顾了不确定性感知的多准则决策方法，提出了任务导向的分类法，涵盖了问题设置、权重确定、准则间结构建模、解决方案程序等核心方面，并为方法选择提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策常受模糊性、信息不完整、异构数据和专家意见冲突等不确定性因素影响，需要系统梳理不确定性感知的多准则决策方法，为研究者和实践者提供清晰的框架和选择指南。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，构建任务导向的分类法，从问题层面设置、权重确定、准则间结构和因果关系建模、解决方案程序等多个维度系统梳理现有方法，并分析典型输入、核心计算步骤和主要输出。

Result: 提出了一个全面的不确定性感知MCDM分类框架，涵盖了离散、群体/共识、动态、多阶段、多层次、多智能体和多场景等七种问题设置，对比了补偿性评分、距离参考和妥协方法、非补偿性排序框架等解决方案，并提供了基于方法鲁棒性、可解释性和数据可用性的选择指导。

Conclusion: 该调查为不确定性感知MCDM领域提供了系统性的梳理和分类框架，指出了未来研究方向，包括可解释的不确定性集成、稳定性分析以及大规模动态决策环境中的可扩展性问题。

Abstract: Decision-making in real applications is often affected by vagueness, incomplete information, heterogeneous data, and conflicting expert opinions. This survey reviews uncertainty-aware multi-criteria decision-making (MCDM) and organizes the field into a concise, task-oriented taxonomy. We summarize problem-level settings (discrete, group/consensus, dynamic, multi-stage, multi-level, multiagent, and multi-scenario), weight elicitation (subjective and objective schemes under fuzzy/linguistic inputs), and inter-criteria structure and causality modelling. For solution procedures, we contrast compensatory scoring methods, distance-to-reference and compromise approaches, and non-compensatory outranking frameworks for ranking or sorting. We also outline rule/evidence-based and sequential decision models that produce interpretable rules or policies. The survey highlights typical inputs, core computational steps, and primary outputs, and provides guidance on choosing methods according to robustness, interpretability, and data availability. It concludes with open directions on explainable uncertainty integration, stability, and scalability in large-scale and dynamic decision environments.

</details>


### [245] [Context-Length Robustness in Question Answering Models: A Comparative Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15723)
*Trishita Dhara,Siddhesh Sheth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过控制实验评估大语言模型在长噪声上下文中的鲁棒性，发现随着上下文长度增加，模型性能持续下降，多跳推理任务比单跨度抽取任务下降更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地部署在长噪声上下文中，模型对上下文长度增长的鲁棒性在不同问答任务中仍缺乏深入理解，需要系统评估模型在长上下文环境下的可靠性。

Method: 使用SQuAD和HotpotQA两个基准数据集进行控制实验，通过系统增加无关上下文长度同时保持答案信号不变，评估模型准确率随总上下文长度的变化，从而隔离上下文长度与任务难度的影响。

Result: 结果显示随着上下文长度增加，模型性能持续下降，HotpotQA（多跳推理任务）的准确率下降幅度几乎是SQuAD（单跨度抽取任务）的两倍，表明多跳推理对上下文稀释特别脆弱。

Conclusion: 上下文长度鲁棒性应在评估模型可靠性时被明确考虑，特别是对于涉及长文档或检索增强生成的应用，多跳推理任务需要特别关注上下文稀释问题。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings where relevant information is embedded within long and noisy contexts. Despite this, robustness to growing context length remains poorly understood across different question answering tasks. In this work, we present a controlled empirical study of context-length robustness in large language models using two widely used benchmarks: SQuAD and HotpotQA.
  We evaluate model accuracy as a function of total context length by systematically increasing the amount of irrelevant context while preserving the answer-bearing signal. This allows us to isolate the effect of context length from changes in task difficulty. Our results show a consistent degradation in performance as context length increases, with substantially larger drops observed on multi-hop reasoning tasks compared to single-span extraction tasks. In particular, HotpotQA exhibits nearly twice the accuracy degradation of SQuAD under equivalent context expansions.
  These findings highlight task-dependent differences in robustness and suggest that multi-hop reasoning is especially vulnerable to context dilution. We argue that context-length robustness should be evaluated explicitly when assessing model reliability, especially for applications involving long documents or retrieval-augmented generation.

</details>


### [246] [CUBE: A Standard for Unifying Agent Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15798)
*Alexandre Lacoste,Nicolas Gontier,Oleh Shliazhko,Aman Jaiswal,Kusha Sareen,Shailesh Nanisetty,Joan Cabezas,Manuel Del Verme,Omar G. Younis,Simone Baratta,Matteo Avalle,Imene Kerboua,Xing Han Lù,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Asaf Yehudai,Leshem Choshen,Jonathan Lebensold,Sean Hughes,Massimo Caccia,Alexandre Drouin,Siva Reddy,Tao Yu,Yu Su,Graham Neubig,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CUBE是一个基于MCP和Gym的通用基准测试协议标准，旨在解决AI智能体基准测试碎片化问题，通过统一封装实现"一次封装，到处使用"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体基准测试的快速扩散导致了严重的碎片化问题，每个新基准都需要大量定制集成工作，形成了"集成税"，限制了全面评估能力，威胁研究生产力。

Method: 提出CUBE（Common Unified Benchmark Environments）协议标准，基于MCP和Gym构建，将任务、基准、包和注册表关注点分离到不同的API层，使任何兼容平台都能访问任何兼容基准。

Result: CUBE允许基准测试被封装一次后在任何兼容平台上使用，支持评估、强化学习训练和数据生成，无需定制集成，解决了基准测试碎片化问题。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区在2026年基准测试生产加速之前共同开发这一标准，以防止平台特定实现加深碎片化，促进研究生产力的提升。

Abstract: The proliferation of agent benchmarks has created critical fragmentation that threatens research productivity. Each new benchmark requires substantial custom integration, creating an "integration tax" that limits comprehensive evaluation. We propose CUBE (Common Unified Benchmark Environments), a universal protocol standard built on MCP and Gym that allows benchmarks to be wrapped once and used everywhere. By separating task, benchmark, package, and registry concerns into distinct API layers, CUBE enables any compliant platform to access any compliant benchmark for evaluation, RL training, or data generation without custom integration. We call on the community to contribute to the development of this standard before platform-specific implementations deepen fragmentation as benchmark production accelerates through 2026.

</details>


### [247] [Prose2Policy (P2P): A Practical LLM Pipeline for Translating Natural-Language Access Policies into Executable Rego](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15799)
*Vatsal Gupta,Darshan Sreenivasamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Prose2Policy (P2P) 是一个基于LLM的工具，可将自然语言访问控制策略自动转换为可执行的Rego代码，提供端到端处理流程并确保部署可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自然语言访问控制策略与机器可执行策略代码之间的鸿沟，强调部署可靠性和可审计性，为零信任和合规驱动环境提供自动化解决方案。

Method: 采用模块化端到端管道，包含策略检测、组件提取、模式验证、代码检查、编译、自动测试生成和执行等步骤，基于LLM技术实现自然语言到Rego代码的转换。

Result: 在ACRE数据集上评估显示：95.3%的接受策略编译成功，自动测试获得82.2%的正测试通过率和98.9%的负测试通过率，表明生成的Rego代码在语法和行为上均表现稳健。

Conclusion: Prose2Policy能够生成语法健壮且行为一致的Rego策略，适用于零信任和合规驱动环境，有效弥合了人类可读访问需求与机器可执行策略代码之间的差距。

Abstract: Prose2Policy (P2P) is a LLM-based practical tool that translates natural-language access control policies (NLACPs) into executable Rego code (the policy language of Open Policy Agent, OPA). It provides a modular, end-to-end pipeline that performs policy detection, component extraction, schema validation, linting, compilation, automatic test generation and execution. Prose2Policy is designed to bridge the gap between human-readable access requirements and machine-enforceable policy-as-code (PaC) while emphasizing deployment reliability and auditability. We evaluated Prose2Policy on the ACRE dataset and demonstrated a 95.3\% compile rate for accepted policies, with automated testing achieving a 82.2\% positive-test pass rate and a 98.9\% negative-test pass rate. These results indicate that Prose2Policy produces syntactically robust and behaviorally consistent Rego policies suitable for Zero Trust and compliance-driven environments.

</details>


### [248] [Persona-Conditioned Risk Behavior in Large Language Models: A Simulated Gambling Study with GPT-4.1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15831)
*Sankalp Dubedy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GPT-4在不同社会经济角色下表现出符合前景理论的风险决策模式：贫困角色赌博次数显著多于富裕角色，风险偏好差异显著，情绪标签仅事后标注而非决策驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型在不确定序列决策环境中的行为是否反映原则性认知模式，还是仅仅是表面提示模仿；验证经典认知经济偏见是否隐含编码于预训练语言模型中。

Method: 控制实验：为GPT-4分配三种社会经济角色（富裕、中等收入、贫困），置于三种老虎机配置环境（公平50%、偏置低35%、连胜动态概率）；每种条件50次独立迭代，记录6950个决策；分析行为特征、风险评分、情绪标签作用和信念更新。

Result: 贫困角色平均玩37.4轮（SD=15.5），富裕角色仅1.1轮（SD=0.31），差异极显著（Kruskal-Wallis H=393.5, p<2.2e-16）；风险评分效应量大（Cohen's d=4.15）；情绪标签仅事后标注而非决策驱动（chi-square=3205.4, Cramer's V=0.39）；信念更新可忽略（Spearman rho=0.032）。

Conclusion: GPT-4在未明确指示情况下复现了卡尼曼和特沃斯基前景理论的关键行为特征，表明经典认知经济偏见可能隐含编码于大规模预训练语言模型中，对LLM智能体设计、可解释性研究有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in uncertain, sequential decision-making contexts. Yet it remains poorly understood whether the behaviors they exhibit in such environments reflect principled cognitive patterns or simply surface-level prompt mimicry. This paper presents a controlled experiment in which GPT-4.1 was assigned one of three socioeconomic personas (Rich, Middle-income, and Poor) and placed in a structured slot-machine environment with three distinct machine configurations: Fair (50%), Biased Low (35%), and Streak (dynamic probability increasing after consecutive losses). Across 50 independent iterations per condition and 6,950 recorded decisions, we find that the model reproduces key behavioral signatures predicted by Kahneman and Tversky's Prospect Theory without being instructed to do so. The Poor persona played a mean of 37.4 rounds per session (SD=15.5) compared to 1.1 rounds for the Rich persona (SD=0.31), a difference that is highly significant (Kruskal-Wallis H=393.5, p<2.2e-16). Risk scores by persona show large effect sizes (Cohen's d=4.15 for Poor vs Rich). Emotional labels appear to function as post-hoc annotations rather than decision drivers (chi-square=3205.4, Cramer's V=0.39), and belief-updating across rounds is negligible (Spearman rho=0.032 for Poor persona, p=0.016). These findings carry implications for LLM agent design, interpretability research, and the broader question of whether classical cognitive economic biases are implicitly encoded in large-scale pretrained language models.

</details>


### [249] [Algorithmic Trading Strategy Development and Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15848)
*Owen Nyo Wei Yuan,Victor Tan Jia Xuan,Ong Jun Yao Fabian,Ryan Tan Jun Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种增强型算法交易策略，通过整合S&P 500历史数据、技术指标和基于FinBERT的财报电话会议情感分析来优化交易决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统算法交易策略可能未充分利用市场情感信息，特别是财报电话会议等文本数据中的情感信号。研究旨在探索结合技术分析和情感分析能否提升交易策略性能。

Method: 使用S&P 500历史市场数据，整合移动平均线、动量、波动率等技术指标，并采用FinBERT模型对财报电话会议进行情感分析。通过计算优化将这些因素结合到交易策略中。

Result: 增强策略在总回报率、夏普比率和最大回撤等多个指标上显著优于基准模型，证明了技术指标与情感分析结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合技术指标、情感分析和计算优化能有效提升算法交易系统性能，证明了多源信息融合在金融交易中的价值和相关性。

Abstract: The report presents with the development and optimisation of an enhanced algorithmic trading strategy through the use of historical S&P 500 market data and earnings call sentiment analysis. The proposed strategy integrates various technical indicators such as moving averages, momentum, volatility, and FinBERT-based sentiment analysis to improve overall trades being taken. The results show that the enhanced strategy significantly outperforms the baseline model in terms of total return, Sharpe ratio, and drawdown amongst other factors. The findings helped demonstrate the relevance and effectiveness of combining technical indicators, sentiment analysis, and computational optimisation in algorithmic trading systems.

</details>


### [250] [Regularized Latent Dynamics Prediction is a Strong Baseline For Behavioral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15857)
*Pranaya Jajoo,Harshit Sikchi,Siddhant Agarwal,Amy Zhang,Scott Niekum,Martha White*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RLDP通过简单的正交正则化提升零样本强化学习中的状态特征多样性，在低覆盖场景下优于复杂表征学习方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为基础模型(BFMs)需要复杂的目标函数和充分的数据覆盖来学习有用的状态特征，限制了零样本强化学习的表达能力和实用性

Method: 提出正则化潜在动态预测(RLDP)，在潜在空间自监督下一状态预测的基础上，添加正交正则化来保持特征多样性，防止特征相似性增加

Result: RLDP能够匹配或超越最先进的复杂表征学习方法，在低覆盖场景下表现尤为突出，而先前方法在此类场景中表现不佳

Conclusion: 简单的正交正则化结合下一状态预测足以实现有效的零样本强化学习，无需复杂的表征学习目标，特别是在数据覆盖有限的情况下

Abstract: Behavioral Foundation Models (BFMs) produce agents with the capability to adapt to any unknown reward or task. These methods, however, are only able to produce near-optimal policies for the reward functions that are in the span of some pre-existing state features, making the choice of state features crucial to the expressivity of the BFM. As a result, BFMs are trained using a variety of complex objectives and require sufficient dataset coverage, to train task-useful spanning features. In this work, we examine the question: are these complex representation learning objectives necessary for zero-shot RL? Specifically, we revisit the objective of self-supervised next-state prediction in latent space for state feature learning, but observe that such an objective alone is prone to increasing state-feature similarity, and subsequently reducing span. We propose an approach, Regularized Latent Dynamics Prediction (RLDP), that adds a simple orthogonality regularization to maintain feature diversity and can match or surpass state-of-the-art complex representation learning methods for zero-shot RL. Furthermore, we empirically show that prior approaches perform poorly in low-coverage scenarios where RLDP still succeeds.

</details>


### [251] [Resilience Meets Autonomy: Governing Embodied AI in Critical Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15885)
*Puneet Sharma,Christer Henrik Pursiainen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出在关键基础设施中，具身AI的韧性依赖于混合治理架构中的有限自主性，并基于任务复杂性、风险水平和后果严重性为不同基础设施部门设计了四种监督模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施越来越多地采用具身AI进行监控、预测性维护和决策支持，但这些AI系统在处理超出其训练假设的级联故障和危机动态时存在困难，需要建立有效的治理框架来确保其韧性。

Method: 论文提出了混合治理架构中的有限自主性概念，基于欧盟AI法案、ISO安全标准和危机管理研究，设计了四种监督模式，并根据任务复杂性、风险水平和后果严重性将这些模式映射到不同的关键基础设施部门。

Result: 论文建立了具身AI在关键基础设施中的治理框架，通过结构化分配机器能力和人类判断，为不同基础设施部门提供了针对性的监督模式，以增强系统在危机情况下的韧性。

Conclusion: 具身AI在关键基础设施中的有效治理需要混合架构，结合有限自主性和结构化的人类监督，通过基于任务特性、风险水平和后果严重性的监督模式分配，才能确保系统在面对超出训练假设的危机时的韧性。

Abstract: Critical infrastructure increasingly incorporates embodied AI for monitoring, predictive maintenance, and decision support. However, AI systems designed to handle statistically representable uncertainty struggle with cascading failures and crisis dynamics that exceed their training assumptions. This paper argues that Embodied AIs resilience depends on bounded autonomy within a hybrid governance architecture. We outline four oversight modes and map them to critical infrastructure sectors based on task complexity, risk level, and consequence severity. Drawing on the EU AI Act, ISO safety standards, and crisis management research, we argue that effective governance requires a structured allocation of machine capability and human judgement.

</details>


### [252] [AsgardBench - Evaluating Visually Grounded Interactive Planning Under Minimal Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15888)
*Andrea Tupini,Lars Liden,Reuben Tan,Yu Wang,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AsgardBench是一个评估视觉基础的高层动作序列生成和交互式规划的基准，专注于执行过程中基于视觉观察的计划适应，而非导航或低层操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI基准要么将推理与导航混为一谈，要么提供丰富的纠正反馈替代感知。AsgardBench旨在隔离交互式规划能力，要求智能体仅基于图像、动作历史和轻量级成功/失败信号进行计划修订，在受控模拟器中评估视觉基础的计划适应能力。

Method: 基准包含108个任务实例，涵盖12种任务类型，通过对象状态、放置和场景配置进行系统变化。这些受控变化创建了条件分支，使得单一指令可能需要不同的动作序列，强调执行过程中的条件分支和计划修复。智能体输入仅限于图像、动作历史和轻量级成功/失败信号。

Result: 对领先的视觉语言模型的评估显示，在没有视觉输入的情况下性能急剧下降，揭示了视觉基础和状态跟踪的弱点，这些弱点最终会破坏交互式规划能力。

Conclusion: AsgardBench聚焦于一个核心问题：模型能否真正利用所见内容在事情未按预期发展时调整计划？该基准通过隔离交互式规划能力，为评估视觉基础的计划适应提供了专门工具。

Abstract: With AsgardBench we aim to evaluate visually grounded, high-level action sequence generation and interactive planning, focusing specifically on plan adaptation during execution based on visual observations rather than navigation or low-level manipulation. In the landscape of embodied AI benchmarks, AsgardBench targets the capability category of interactive planning, which is more sophisticated than offline high-level planning as it requires agents to revise plans in response to environmental feedback, yet remains distinct from low-level execution.
  Unlike prior embodied AI benchmarks that conflate reasoning with navigation or provide rich corrective feedback that substitutes for perception, AsgardBench restricts agent input to images, action history, and lightweight success/failure signals, isolating interactive planning in a controlled simulator without low-level control noise.
  The benchmark contains 108 task instances spanning 12 task types, each systematically varied through object state, placement, and scene configuration. These controlled variations create conditional branches in which a single instruction can require different action sequences depending on what the agent observes, emphasizing conditional branching and plan repair during execution.
  Our evaluations of leading vision language models show that performance drops sharply without visual input, revealing weaknesses in visual grounding and state tracking that ultimately undermine interactive planning. Our benchmark zeroes in on a narrower question: can a model actually use what it sees to adapt a plan when things do not go as expected?

</details>


### [253] [Argumentative Human-AI Decision-Making: Toward AI Agents That Reason With Us, Not For Us](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15946)
*Stylianos Loukas Vasileiou,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni,William Yeoh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 计算论证与大型语言模型融合，构建可辩论、可修正的人机协同决策新范式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算论证框架具有透明可验证的优点但依赖领域知识和特征工程，而LLMs擅长处理非结构化文本但推理过程不透明。两者结合可创建既具备形式化推理透明度又能处理复杂自然语言的人机协同决策系统。

Method: 通过论证框架挖掘、论证框架合成和论证推理三个关键技术的协同，构建能够参与辩证过程、使决策可辩论和可修订的智能体系统。

Result: 提出了"论证性人机决策"新范式，该范式通过计算论证的形式化框架与LLMs的自然语言处理能力相结合，实现推理过程透明、决策可辩论、结果可修订的人机协同决策。

Conclusion: 计算论证与LLMs的融合对于高风险领域构建人类可理解、可信赖的AI系统至关重要，能够实现"与人推理而非为人推理"的辩证决策过程。

Abstract: Computational argumentation offers formal frameworks for transparent, verifiable reasoning but has traditionally been limited by its reliance on domain-specific information and extensive feature engineering. In contrast, LLMs excel at processing unstructured text, yet their opaque nature makes their reasoning difficult to evaluate and trust. We argue that the convergence of these fields will lay the foundation for a new paradigm: Argumentative Human-AI Decision-Making. We analyze how the synergy of argumentation framework mining, argumentation framework synthesis, and argumentative reasoning enables agents that do not just justify decisions, but engage in dialectical processes where decisions are contestable and revisable -- reasoning with humans rather than for them. This convergence of computational argumentation and LLMs is essential for human-aware, trustworthy AI in high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [254] [Optimizing Hospital Capacity During Pandemics: A Dual-Component Framework for Strategic Patient Relocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15960)
*Sadaf Tabatabaee,Hicham El Baz,Mohammed Khalil Ghali,Nagendra N. Nagarur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个结合时间序列预测和仿真建模的两部分框架，通过患者转移策略优化医院容量，以应对COVID-19疫情期间的医疗系统压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COVID-19大流行给全球医院系统带来巨大压力，导致关键容量挑战，需要有效策略来优化医院容量和患者流动。

Method: 开发两部分框架：1) 基于历史COVID-19病例和住院数据的时间序列预测模型，用于预测患者到达率；2) 仿真模型评估不同患者转移策略的影响，考虑床位可用性、人员能力、运输物流和患者病情严重程度等因素。

Result: 该框架将为医院管理者提供全面的决策支持工具，使他们能够预测需求、模拟转移策略并实施优化政策，以分配患者和资源。

Conclusion: 通过结合预测分析和仿真建模，该研究旨在增强医疗系统在面对COVID-19和未来大流行时的韧性，提高医院容量管理的效率和效果。

Abstract: The COVID-19 pandemic has placed immense strain on hospital systems worldwide, leading to critical capacity challenges. This research proposes a two-part framework to optimize hospital capacity through patient relocation strategies. The first component involves developing a time series prediction model to forecast patient arrival rates. Using historical data on COVID-19 cases and hospitalizations, the model will generate accurate forecasts of future patient volumes. This will enable hospitals to proactively plan resource allocation and patient flow. The second com- ponent is a simulation model that evaluates the impact of different patient relocation strategies. The simulation will account for factors such as bed availability, staff capabilities, transportation logistics, and patient acuity to optimize the placement of patients across networked hospitals. Multiple scenarios will be tested, including inter-hospital trans- fers, use of temporary care facilities, and adaptations to discharge protocols. By combining predictive analytics and simulation modeling, this research aims to provide hospital administrators with a comprehensive decision-support tool. The proposed framework will empower them to anticipate demand, simulate relocation strategies, and imple- ment optimal policies to distribute patients and resources. Ultimately, this work seeks to enhance the resilience of healthcare systems in the face of COVID-19 and future pandemics.

</details>


### [255] [Safety is Non-Compositional: A Formal Framework for Capability-Based AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15973)
*Cosimo Spera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次证明在存在合取能力依赖时，安全性不具备组合性：两个单独无法达到任何禁止能力的智能体，组合后可通过突现的合取依赖共同达到禁止目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多智能体系统中安全性的组合性质，特别是在存在合取能力依赖的情况下。传统上可能假设如果每个组件单独是安全的，那么组合系统也应该是安全的，但本文旨在证明这种假设在特定条件下不成立。

Method: 采用形式化证明方法，分析合取能力依赖（conjunctive capability dependencies）对安全性的影响。通过构建理论模型，展示两个单独安全的智能体如何通过组合产生新的能力依赖关系，从而违反安全约束。

Result: 证明了安全性在合取能力依赖存在时不具备组合性。具体展示了两个单独无法达到任何禁止能力的智能体，当组合在一起时，能够通过突现的合取依赖关系共同达到原本禁止的目标能力。

Conclusion: 安全性的组合性在存在合取能力依赖时失效，这对多智能体系统的安全验证具有重要意义。需要开发新的方法来分析和确保组合系统的安全性，不能仅依赖组件级别的安全保证。

Abstract: This paper contains the first formal proof that safety is non-compositional in the presence of conjunctive capability dependencies: two agents each individually inca- pable of reaching any forbidden capability can, when combined, collectively reach a forbidden goal through an emergent conjunctive dependency.

</details>


### [256] [An Agentic Evaluation Framework for AI-Generated Scientific Code in PETSc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15976)
*Hong Zhang,Barry Smith,Satish Balay,Le Chen,Murat Keceli,Lois Curfman McInnes,Junchao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了petscagent-bench框架，采用"智能体评估智能体"范式，通过14个评估器在5个维度上全面评估HPC科学代码生成质量，弥补传统测试用例匹配方法的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准测试仅通过测试用例匹配评估代码生成，对于HPC库代码来说过于简单，无法评估求解器选择、API约定、内存管理和性能等关键维度，需要更全面的评估框架。

Method: 构建基于智能体评估智能体范式的petscagent-bench框架，部署工具增强的评估器智能体，通过14个评估器管道在5个评分类别（正确性、性能、代码质量、算法适当性、库特定约定）上编译、执行和测量被测模型生成的代码。

Result: 在PETSc库的HPC实际问题上进行实证分析，发现当前前沿模型能生成可读性好、结构良好的代码，但在库特定约定方面持续存在困难，这是传统通过/失败指标完全无法检测到的。

Conclusion: petscagent-bench框架通过智能体间的标准化协议通信实现了黑盒评估，为科学代码生成提供了更全面、多维度的评估方法，揭示了传统评估方法的局限性。

Abstract: While large language models have significantly accelerated scientific code generation, comprehensively evaluating the generated code remains a major challenge. Traditional benchmarks reduce evaluation to test-case matching, an approach insufficient for library code in HPC where solver selection, API conventions, memory management, and performance are just as critical as functional correctness. To address this gap, we introduce petscagent-bench, an agentic framework built on an agents-evaluating-agents paradigm. Instead of relying on static scripts, petscagent-bench deploys a tool-augmented evaluator agent that compiles, executes, and measures code produced by a separate model-under-test agent, orchestrating a 14-evaluator pipeline across five scoring categories: correctness, performance, code quality, algorithmic appropriateness, and library-specific conventions. Because the agents communicate through standardized protocols (A2A and MCP), the framework enables black-box evaluation of any coding agent without requiring access to its source code. We demonstrate the framework on a benchmark suite of realistic problems using the PETSc library for HPC. Our empirical analysis of frontier models reveals that while current models generate readable, well-structured code, they consistently struggle with library-specific conventions that traditional pass/fail metrics completely miss.

</details>


### [257] [Selective Memory for Artificial Intelligence: Write-Time Gating with Hierarchical Archiving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15994)
*Oliver Zahn,Simran Chana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于写入时门控的检索增强生成方法，通过复合显著性评分过滤知识对象并维护版本链，在干扰物比例增加时显著优于读取时过滤方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成方法存在两个问题：1）无差别存储所有内容导致噪声积累降低准确性；2）参数化方法将知识压缩到权重中，无法进行选择性更新。这两种方法都无法模拟生物记忆的机制，即基于显著性进行编码门控并归档而非删除过时信息。

Method: 提出写入时门控机制，使用复合显著性评分（来源声誉、新颖性、可靠性）过滤传入的知识对象，同时维护版本链以保留先前状态。该方法在真实LLM评估中无需访问质量标签的oracle，通过信号消融验证不依赖与oracle相关的元数据。

Result: 写入时门控在无干扰情况下达到100%准确率（未门控存储为13%）。在8:1干扰物比例下，读取时过滤（Self-RAG）准确率降至0%，而写入时门控保持100%。该方法在Wikipedia（20个实体）、程序生成药理学数据和2026年arXiv论文上验证有效，且写入时门控优势与参数化记忆支持成反比：Wikipedia +25pp，截止后arXiv +48pp，零训练知识的程序数据 +65pp。写入时门控以九分之一的查询时间成本达到Self-RAG的准确率。

Conclusion: 写入时门控在干扰物比例增加时展现出相对于读取时过滤的结构性优势，能够有效过滤噪声并维护知识版本历史，为检索增强生成系统提供了更接近生物记忆机制的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation stores all content indiscriminately, degrading accuracy as noise accumulates. Parametric approaches compress knowledge into weights, precluding selective updates. Neither mirrors biological memory, which gates encoding based on salience and archives rather than deletes superseded information. We introduce write-time gating that filters incoming knowledge objects using composite salience scores (source reputation, novelty, reliability) while maintaining version chains that preserve prior states. Using real LLM evaluation without oracle access to quality labels, write gating achieves 100 percent accuracy versus 13 percent for ungated stores. The critical finding emerges under distractor scaling: at 8:1 distractor ratios, read-time filtering (Self-RAG) collapses to 0 percent while write gating maintains 100 percent, revealing a structural advantage of write-time over read-time curation. Validation on Wikipedia (20 entities), procedurally generated pharmacology data, and 2026 arXiv papers confirms these findings. The gating advantage scales inversely with parametric memory support: +25pp for Wikipedia, +48pp for post-cutoff arXiv, +65pp for procedural data with zero training knowledge. Signal ablation confirms the method does not depend on oracle-correlated metadata. Write gating matches Self-RAG accuracy at one-ninth the query-time cost.

</details>


### [258] [Enhancing Linguistic Generalization of VLA: Fine-Tuning OpenVLA via Synthetic Instruction Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16044)
*Dongik Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出参数高效微调策略，通过LLM生成语义等价但结构多样的指令集增强OpenVLA的语言泛化能力，在Bridge Dataset V2上实现性能提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OpenVLA在视觉-语言-动作模型中达到SOTA水平，但在面对全新环境时的零样本性能仍然有限，需要提升其语言泛化能力以适应多样化环境

Method: 1) 使用大型语言模型为Bridge Dataset V2中的现有轨迹生成语义等价但结构多样的指令集；2) 采用低秩适应(LoRA)对OpenVLA在增强的指令-动作对上进行参数高效微调

Result: LoRA增强模型展现出更强的鲁棒性，表明丰富专用数据集的语义空间对于具身智能体至关重要，能够弥合复杂自然语言意图与机器人动作之间的鸿沟

Conclusion: 通过参数高效微调和语言指令增强策略，能够显著提升视觉-语言-动作模型在具身AI任务中的语言泛化能力，为适应多样化环境提供有效解决方案

Abstract: Generalization remains a core challenge in embodied AI, as robots must adapt to diverse environments. While OpenVLA represents the State-of-the-Art (SOTA) in Vision-Language-Action models by leveraging large-scale pre-training, its zero-shot performance can be limited when encountering completely new environments. This paper proposes a parameter-efficient fine-tuning strategy to enhance the linguistic generalization of OpenVLA by synthesizing a general instruction set for the Bridge Dataset V2. The paper leverages a Large Language Model (LLM) to generate a rich variety of semantically equivalent but structurally diverse commands for existing trajectories. In this experiment, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is implemented to fine-tune OpenVLA on augmented pairs, allowing the model to bridge the gap between complex natural language intent and robotic actions. Results demonstrate that the LoRA-enhanced model's robustness, suggesting that enriching the linguistic space of specialized datasets is crucial for embodied agents.

</details>


### [259] [POaaS: Minimal-Edit Prompt Optimization as a Service to Lift Accuracy and Cut Hallucinations on On-Device sLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16045)
*Jungwoo Shim,Dae Won Kim,Sun Wook Kim,Soo Young Kim,Myungcheol Lee,Jae-geun Cha,Hyunhwa Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: POaaS是一种针对设备端小语言模型的轻量级提示优化方法，通过路由到专用模块进行最小编辑，相比传统自动提示优化方法在保持准确性的同时减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端小语言模型面临用户提示不完善（拼写错误、意图不清、上下文缺失）导致的事实错误和幻觉问题。现有的自动提示优化方法为云端大模型设计，依赖搜索生成冗长结构化指令，在设备端同一小模型同时作为优化器和求解器时浪费上下文甚至损害准确性。

Method: 提出POaaS（最小编辑提示优化层），将每个查询路由到轻量级专家模块（Cleaner清理器、Paraphraser改写器、Fact-Adder事实添加器），在严格的漂移和长度约束下合并输出，对格式良好的提示采用保守跳过策略。

Result: 在严格的固定模型设置下（Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct），POaaS提高了任务准确性和事实性，而代表性APO基线方法反而降低性能。在token删除和混合情况下，POaaS恢复性能提升达+7.4%。

Conclusion: 针对设备端小语言模型，基于每查询的保守优化是搜索密集型自动提示优化的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Small language models (sLLMs) are increasingly deployed on-device, where imperfect user prompts--typos, unclear intent, or missing context--can trigger factual errors and hallucinations. Existing automatic prompt optimization (APO) methods were designed for large cloud LLMs and rely on search that often produces long, structured instructions; when executed under an on-device constraint where the same small model must act as optimizer and solver, these pipelines can waste context and even hurt accuracy. We propose POaaS, a minimal-edit prompt optimization layer that routes each query to lightweight specialists (Cleaner, Paraphraser, Fact-Adder) and merges their outputs under strict drift and length constraints, with a conservative skip policy for well-formed prompts. Under a strict fixed-model setting with Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, POaaS improves both task accuracy and factuality while representative APO baselines degrade them, and POaaS recovers up to +7.4% under token deletion and mixup. Overall, per-query conservative optimization is a practical alternative to search-heavy APO for on-device sLLMs.

</details>


### [260] [A Context Alignment Pre-processor for Enhancing the Coherence of Human-LLM Dialog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16052)
*Ding Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Context Alignment Pre-processor (C.A.P.)框架，作为LLM对话中的预处理模块，解决上下文对齐问题，通过语义扩展、时间加权上下文检索和对齐验证来改善长时动态对话质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生成流畅文本方面取得显著进展，但在长时动态对话中面临上下文对齐的关键挑战。当用户在对话中省略前提、简化指代或突然切换上下文时，模型可能无法捕捉用户真实意图，产生机械或离题的回答，削弱对话的协作潜力。

Method: 提出C.A.P.框架作为用户输入和响应生成之间的预处理模块，包含三个核心过程：1) 语义扩展，将用户指令扩展到更广泛的语义范围，包括前提、字面意义和隐含含义；2) 时间加权上下文检索，通过时间衰减函数优先考虑最近的对话历史，近似人类对话焦点；3) 对齐验证和决策分支，通过测量当前提示与加权历史上下文之间的语义相似度来评估对话是否保持在正轨上，当检测到显著偏差时启动结构化澄清协议。

Result: 该研究提出了C.A.P.的架构和理论基础，借鉴了认知科学和人机交互中的共同基础理论。C.A.P.不仅是一种技术改进，也是将人机对话从单向命令执行模式转向双向、自我纠正、基于伙伴关系的协作的重要一步。

Conclusion: 讨论了实现路径、评估方法以及对未来交互式智能系统设计的影响。C.A.P.框架为解决LLM在动态对话中的上下文对齐问题提供了系统性的解决方案，有望提升人机协作对话的质量和效果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made remarkable progress in generating fluent text, but they still face a critical challenge of contextual misalignment in long-term and dynamic dialogue. When human users omit premises, simplify references, or shift context abruptly during interactions with LLMs, the models may fail to capture their actual intentions, producing mechanical or off-topic responses that weaken the collaborative potential of dialogue. To address this problem, this paper proposes a computational framework called the Context Alignment Pre-processor (C.A.P.). Rather than operating during generation, C.A.P. functions as a pre-processing module between user input and response generation. The framework includes three core processes: (1) semantic expansion, which extends a user instruction to a broader semantic span including its premises, literal meaning, and implications; (2) time-weighted context retrieval, which prioritizes recent dialogue history through a temporal decay function approximating human conversational focus; and (3) alignment verification and decision branching, which evaluates whether the dialogue remains on track by measuring the semantic similarity between the current prompt and the weighted historical context. When a significant deviation is detected, C.A.P. initiates a structured clarification protocol to help users and the system recalibrate the conversation. This study presents the architecture and theoretical basis of C.A.P., drawing on cognitive science and Common Ground theory in human-computer interaction. We argue that C.A.P. is not only a technical refinement but also a step toward shifting human-computer dialogue from one-way command-execution patterns to two-way, self-correcting, partnership-based collaboration. Finally, we discuss implementation paths, evaluation methods, and implications for the future design of interactive intelligent systems.

</details>


### [261] [ARISE: Agent Reasoning with Intrinsic Skill Evolution in Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16060)
*Yu Li,Rui Miao,Zhengling Qi,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARISE是一个分层强化学习框架，通过构建可重用的技能库来提升语言模型的数学推理能力，相比现有方法在分布外任务上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法孤立处理每个数学问题，未能充分利用训练过程中积累的可重用推理策略。需要一种能够捕获和复用成功解题轨迹中结构化知识的框架。

Method: 提出分层强化学习框架ARISE，包含技能管理器（高层）和工作器（低层）。管理器通过专门的技能生成rollout对成功解题轨迹进行结构化总结，构建分层技能库，并在执行前使用策略驱动机制检索相关技能来指导后续推理。

Result: 在两个基础模型和七个基准测试（包括竞赛数学和Omni-MATH）上，ARISE一致优于GRPO系列算法和记忆增强基线，在分布外任务上表现尤为突出。消融研究证实各组件均对改进有贡献，且技能库质量与推理性能在训练中同步提升。

Conclusion: ARISE通过分层强化学习和可重用技能库的协同演化，有效提升了语言模型的数学推理能力，特别是在分布外泛化方面表现优异，为数学推理的持续改进提供了新范式。

Abstract: The dominant paradigm for improving mathematical reasoning in language models relies on Reinforcement Learning with verifiable rewards. Yet existing methods treat each problem instance in isolation without leveraging the reusable strategies that emerge and accumulate during training. To this end, we introduce ARISE (Agent Reasoning via Intrinsic Skill Evolution), a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework, in which a shared policy operates both to manage skills at high-level and to generate responses at low-level (denoted as a Skills Manager and a Worker, respectively).
